- **Image attachments on vision-capable models.** New "Attach &image…" button on the chat tab; click to pick a JPG / PNG / GIF / WebP from disk (8 MB cap), type a message, hit Send. Both desktop 1-on-1 and Telegram DMs are wired up. Telegram groups also handle incoming photos and `image/*` documents — caption becomes the text, `caption_entities` are honored for @-mention gating.
- **Webcam capture — two paths**:
  - **Desktop "T&ake photo" button** (Alt+A) next to Attach. Click → 3-2-1 countdown narrated via NVDA and the activity field → captures from the host webcam, stages the photo as if you'd picked it from disk. opencv-python is an optional dep; missing it produces an actionable error dialog.
  - **`use_webcam` tool** for natural-language requests ("hey, take a photo"). The kin calls the tool, the photo gets captured, and the framework injects a synthetic user turn with the image so the kin actually *sees* the result on its next inference step (rather than only reading a "photo saved" text result).
- **Per-user webcam permission radio** (Settings → Telegram → Users) — Ask each time / Auto-approve / Always deny. The "ask" path pops a wx dialog on the operator's desktop when a Telegram user triggers `use_webcam`, blocking the worker until the operator decides. Layered on top of the existing per-kin tools.json gate and per-user bucket gate.
- **`WebcamApprovalDialog`** with Allow / Deny buttons (Deny is default — safer outcome on a stray Enter keystroke).
- **Capability detection**: `llm_backend.model_supports_images(model)` — reads Ollama `/api/show` capabilities or OpenRouter `architecture.input_modalities`. Attach + Take photo buttons disable when the kin's model can't see; `use_webcam` schema is excluded from non-vision models entirely.
- **Magic-byte image validation** in `kin_persistence.save_attachment{,_bytes}` — refuses files whose contents don't match a known jpg / png / gif / webp header even if the extension or mime_type claims otherwise.
- **`CHANGELOG.md`** — this file.

### Changed
- **Mnemonic cleanup** to resolve conflicts surfaced by the new buttons:
  - "Take photo" → Alt+A (was Alt+T, collided with Tools menu — menu wins).
  - "Settings…" → Alt+E (was Alt+S, collided with Send; pairs with "Edit room…" Alt+E in the other mode panel).
  - "Clear image" → "Re&move image" Alt+M (was Alt+L, collided with Clear chat).
  - "Continue round" → Alt+N (was Alt+O, collided with Load older messages — both visible in room mode).
- `llm_backend.chat()` gains a `kin_name` kwarg so attachment refs (relative paths under the kin's directory) can be expanded to provider-shaped image inputs at send time. OpenRouter gets content-block lists; Ollama gets per-message `images: [base64]` arrays. Threaded through all four chat surfaces (1-on-1, room, Telegram DM, Telegram group).
- `tools.load_tools()` gains a `model` kwarg so capability-gated tools (currently just `use_webcam`) get filtered out when the model can't use the result.
- Regenerate (desktop button + Telegram `/regen`) carries image attachments through — no longer silently downgrades an image turn to text-only on retry.

### Fixed (during pre-release sweep)
- Telegram path used to download incoming images BEFORE the `allow_from` gate. Unauthorized senders could waste bandwidth and have their bytes written to the operator's disk. Download now defers until after all gates.
- Pending attachment state leaked across kin switches — staging an image for kin A then switching to kin B would try to use kin A's file path with kin B. Now cleared in `_load_agent`.
- `_maybe_inject_webcam_turn` returns None when `kin_name` is unset — without this guard, a webcam capture in an edge-case caller would produce a synthetic user turn whose attachment got silently dropped, leaving the model with a "(Here's the photo)" prompt but no image.
- Room tool-loop now passes `kin_name`, so a room kin with `use_webcam` enabled actually sees the captured photo (vs. v1 silent-drop behavior).
- Bot tokens scrubbed from `telegram_failures.log` error strings — `urllib.error` shapes can embed the failing URL in `str(e)` and that URL contains `/bot<TOKEN>/`. `_redact_bot_token` substitutes `/bot[REDACTED]/`.
- `_expand_attachments_for_provider` and `_clean_chat_message` now role-gate to `user` turns only — defense-in-depth against any future code path or manually-edited jsonl placing `attachments` on an assistant / tool / system turn (most providers reject image content blocks on non-user roles).

### Deferred to a follow-up
- **Image attachments in rooms** — by design, room messages with images should @-mention a specific kin so only that kin responds. v1 hides the attach + take-photo buttons in room mode.
- **Multiple images per turn** — data model already supports `attachments: [list]`; v1 UI stages a single image. Multi-attach is a UI-only follow-up.
- **Attachment vacuum** — `agents/<kin>/attachments/` grows monotonically; no auto-prune. Content-addressed by SHA-256 so duplicates dedupe naturally, but distinct images accumulate.
- **Animated GIFs from Telegram** come in as `animation` (not `photo`, not `document`). Out of scope for v1.

---

## v0.2.35 — 2026-05-20

### Added
- **"Chat" tab** in the per-kin Settings dialog (the eighth tab, between Model && generation and Memory). Consolidates the distillation-cadence controls (auto-distill on close + auto-distill every N) that used to live on the Memory tab, paired with a **per-surface counter overview** listing every scope (desktop + each non-shared Telegram DM + each non-shared group) and how many messages each has pending toward the threshold. Each row shows `(X until threshold)` or `← at threshold (will distill on next tick)`.
- **Refresh counters button** on the Chat tab. The dialog is modal so the counter snapshot is from open-time, but Telegram activity ticks counters in the background — this lets the operator force a re-read without closing/reopening.

### Fixed
- Stale `self.frame._messages_since_distill.get(self.kin, 0)` lookup in `_populate_fields` (broken by v0.2.34's tuple-keyed dict — silently returned 0).
- Live Telegram `on_activity` tick now refreshes the Chat tab counter display instead of overwriting the Memory tab's `memory_status` label (which is back to operation-only feedback for the manual buttons).

## v0.2.34 — 2026-05-20

### Added
- **Per-(kin, scope) distillation counters + Telegram-triggered distillation.** Counter dict went from "one number per kin" to "one number per (kin, surface)." Surfaces with share-with-desktop on share a counter (they share storage). Surfaces without share each get their own counter. When any scope trips `memory_distill_every_n`, distillation runs for that scope's history and writes to the shared `memory.md`. New helpers `_distill_scope_for_telegram_user/group`, `_convo_for_distill_scope`. TelegramBot gains an `on_activity(kind, identifier)` callback.

### Fixed
- Pure-Telegram kin (rarely typed in desktop chat) were effectively never distilling — the cadence trigger lived only on the desktop send path. Now every surface ticks independently.

## v0.2.33 — 2026-05-20

### Changed
- **Per-surface read-time filtering for shared groups/DMs.** Storage is unified (group turns live in `conversation.jsonl` with `source=telegram:group:<chat_id>` tags, so a desktop wipe clears them too) but each surface reads ONLY its own slice. Three changes: desktop `_history_entry_for_model` returns None for group-tagged messages; `_load_group_history(chat_id)` filters to ONLY this group's source-tag; `_load_history_for(user_id)` filters out group messages from shared DM threads.

## v0.2.32 — 2026-05-20

### Added
- **Per-group share-with-desktop toggle.** Parallel structure to the per-user `user_share_desktop`. When on for a group, load returns the kin's main `conversation.jsonl`, append writes with `source=telegram:group:<chat_id>` tag, and clear-chat / regen on desktop affects the group too.
- **`_TelegramGroupDialog` Share-with-desktop checkbox** + migration prompt for orphaned group history (mirror of the user-side migration, with `migrate_group_history_to_conversation` + `pop_group_history` for race-free pop-from-bot-under-lock).

## v0.2.31 — 2026-05-20

### Fixed
- **Thinking-content leak on MiMo (and similar models)**. Models that emit reasoning-styled markdown inside `content` itself (no field-level `thinking` separation) bypassed display-time `show_thinking=False` filtering. Fix: thread `show_thinking` through `chat()` → `_build_openrouter_payload`; when `show_thinking=False` AND reasoning is enabled, the payload gets `reasoning.exclude=True` — provider thinks internally but doesn't include the trace in the response. Definitively works for Anthropic / OpenAI o-series / DeepSeek-R1 / Gemini; for models like MiMo that emit reasoning as content style rather than via the reasoning channel, the result depends on provider honoring `exclude`. Threaded through all four desktop helpers, both Telegram paths, and both cron paths.

## v0.2.30 — 2026-05-20

### Fixed
- **Cron-to-Telegram silent typo**: `hearthkin_cron._maybe_post_telegram` read `tg.get("token")` but the config key is `bot_token` everywhere else. Paths 1 (subprocess-only) and 3 (isolated-worker) silently no-op'd since the feature shipped — one-character typo, persistent symptom.
- **Inconsistent cron-recipient rules** across paths. All three cron-to-Telegram paths now use the same opt-in (`user_mirror_to_telegram`).

## v0.2.29 — 2026-05-20

### Fixed
- **Ash "seizure" after tool calls** — `content: null` on assistant turns carrying `tool_calls` was the trigger. The OpenAI streaming spec permits null content there, but Anthropic-via-OpenRouter treats it as a structural defect and the *following* turn's output degenerates. Belt-and-suspenders fix at four layers: `_coerce_tool_call_assistant_content` in `chat()` (universal choke point); `run_tool_loop`'s in-loop assistant assembly stores `""` directly; `_clean_chat_message` heals on-disk null on load; `_history_entry_for_model` emits `""` at the API boundary.
- **`edit_file` parameter naming**: renamed `old`/`new` → `old_string`/`new_string` so models send the right shape on the first call (matching Claude Code's `Edit` tool convention).

## v0.2.28 — 2026-05-20

### Fixed
- **OpenRouter connection-cache race** — two threads acquiring the same host's cached `HTTPSConnection` got the *same* object; the second's `conn.request(...)` raised `http.client.CannotSendRequest('Request-sent')`. Fix: pop-on-acquire / restore-on-success / close-on-failure.
- **Telegram history file race** between Settings dialog migration and bot inference worker. New `_histories_lock` (RLock) on the bot; atomic `pop_user_history(user_id)` method; migration takes optional `bot=` arg.
- **conversation.jsonl write race** between cron isolated worker and a clear-chat / regen from the UI. Per-kin `threading.RLock` in `kin_persistence`; new `save_agent_conversation_preserving_externals(name, conversation, known_count)` reloads disk under the lock, splices external appends, then full-rewrites.

## v0.2.27 — 2026-05-19

### Added
- **OpenRouter balance button** in Preferences → Connections. Hits `/api/v1/credits` and surfaces `total_credits - total_usage` as a plain-text line in the Activity field — accessible alternative to OR's chart-heavy web dashboard.

## v0.2.26 — 2026-05-19

### Fixed
- **Stop-mid-stream save was only stripping timestamps** — the full four-pass anti-impersonation pipeline got skipped, so a stopped turn with a leaked `[OtherKin]:` got persisted intact, feeding the pattern back into next-turn context.
- **2-kin rooms guaranteed consecutive same-speaker pairs at every round boundary** (mathematical property of rotating a 2-element list by 1). Fix: lock rotation to 0 for 2-kin rooms specifically; rotation still works as designed for 3+ kin.

## v0.2.25 — 2026-05-19

### Added
- **`strip_leading_speaker_tag`** — catches any `[Name]:` prefix at the start of a reply, looping for stacked tags. The existing "trifecta" (stop sequence `\n[`, `strip_self_tag`, `strip_trailing_other_speakers`) all required either the kin's own name OR a preceding newline; none caught the most common cross-kin impersonation form (model OPENS its reply with `[OtherKin]:` at position 0).

## v0.2.24 — 2026-05-19

### Fixed
- Telegram bot `stop()` joins worker threads with 1.5s timeout (daemon threads so process exits regardless, but in-flight calls now get to finalize).
- `sendChatAction` typing-indicator failures log once per session instead of silent-forever-or-spam-every-4s.
- All six `save_room_conversation` call sites log on failure (was silently swallowed).
- `llm_backend._truncate_messages` no longer orphans `role=tool` results from their parent assistant turn when truncation drops the parent.
- Room-path streaming watchdog wired in so a hung room kin doesn't leave `_room_active` stuck True forever.

## v0.2.23 — 2026-05-19

### Fixed
- **Stuck `_distilling` set** converted to dict-with-timestamps + 5-minute staleness auto-clear via `_is_distill_in_flight`.
- **Telegram chunking 429 flood**: new `TelegramAPIError` class carrying `status` + `retry_after`; `_send_chunked` honors the hint with capped retries and aborts after 3 consecutive 429s.
- Silent persistence failures in `_append_turns_for` and the cron wake-up paths now log to `telegram_failures.log` / `save_failures.log`.
- Exec denylist `del /f /s /q C:\` pattern was missing its `\s*$` anchor and over-blocked legit `del /f /s /q C:\Temp\...` operations.

## v0.2.22 — 2026-05-19

### Added
- **Per-kin `telegram_token_cap`** config (default 900). The Telegram path was the only conversational surface with NO `num_predict` cap — desktop hardcodes 900, rooms use `per_turn_token_cap` (default 800), Telegram had nothing. Cascade-prone models could generate effectively forever and chunk into 100+ Telegram messages.

## v0.2.21 — 2026-05-19

### Changed
- **Installer pipeline rewrite**: PyInstaller `--onefile` + UPX → `--onedir` + `upx=False`. Plus Inno Setup `[Code] InitializeSetup` taskkills any leftover `Hearthkin.exe` before Setup writes files. Fixes the bootloader-stub-stuck-mid-extraction failure mode that the v0.2.13–v0.2.20 "installer fixes" were addressing from a different (wrong) angle. See `installer/INSTALLER_REWRITE.md` for the full diagnosis.

## v0.2.13 – v0.2.20 — 2026-05-18/19

### Added
- **Telegram group support** (opt-in per chat, mention-only default policy). `[@SenderName]:` attribution lives in a pre-user system note (OpenClaw pattern), not in the message content.
- **Timestamp grounding**: `[YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM]` prefix on user turns only — asymmetric to avoid destabilizing assistant echo loops.
- **`setMyCommands`** auto-published to Telegram so the slash-menu shows the right commands.
- **NVDA slider value announcement** via `nvda_speak` on every change.

### Fixed
- `/whoami` and `/help` bypass `allow_from` (bootstrap commands need to work for unlisted users).
- User turn persists in Telegram history even when model returns empty content.
- Cron subprocess no longer flashes a console window that floods NVDA on every wake-up.
- Cron `schtasks` task definitions no longer bind to ephemeral source location.

## v0.2.11 – v0.2.12 — 2026-05-17

### Added
- **First-run advisory** when Ollama isn't responding.

### Changed
- Installer version derived from git tag instead of being hardcoded.

## v0.2.8 – v0.2.10 — 2026-05-15/16

### Added
- **NVDA Controller Client bundled** (LGPL 2.1, unmodified, shipped external to `Hearthkin.exe` — see `vendor/nvda/README.md`).
- **Streaming watchdog** counts thinking chunks as proof-of-life; threshold raised from 60s to 5min.
- **Settings dialog** (formerly "Edit Kin") with Rename moved into the Identity tab.
- New tool **`recent_thinking`**.
- **Telegram sync diagnostics**: mirror failures log to `telegram_failures.log`; poll reloads speak "Synced N new" via NVDA.
- **Chat render window grows with appends** so the "Load older" count stops lying.

### Fixed
- **`context_status` tool** reads `conversation.jsonl` (was reading the legacy `.json` file and returning 0).
- **Settings dialog**: Show/Feed/Cap stay tab-reachable when Thinking effort is Off (disabled controls weren't tab-reachable on wxMSW).
- **Installer no longer hangs** when Hearthkin is minimized to tray.
- **Auto-update check** logs every outcome to `update_check.log` AND persists the "newer version available" string in the Activity field.

### Changed
- **Build pipeline stamps `__version__`** from the git tag via `scripts/stamp_version.py`. **Do NOT bump `__version__` by hand at tag time** — the prior "edit the constant, then tag" workflow caused two consecutive releases (v0.2.8 and v0.2.9) to self-report stale versions, producing permanent false-positive "update available" notifications.

## v0.2.0 – v0.2.7 — 2026-05-11/14

### Added (highlights)
- **Voice subsystem** (TTS + STT via ElevenLabs). Voice phases A through E: Connections key row → per-kin Voice tab → TTS streaming hook → push-to-talk + Scribe STT → room-mode hiding the Talk button.
- **Phase-2 tools landed**: `tools/` registry with auto-derived schemas, per-kin allowlist files, five initial tools (`memory_search`, `read_file`, `write_file`, `edit_file`, `note`).
- **OpenRouter backend** alongside Ollama (Phase 1). `llm_backend.chat()` is the single dispatch point.
- **Connections panel** in Preferences (per-provider API key entry, masked display, test button).
- **Unified model browser** handles both Ollama and OpenRouter via a provider radio.
- **`exec` tool** with harness-side approval (denylist + per-kin `tool_trust` levels).
- **`fetch_url` tool** with markdown extraction (h1-h6 → `#`, `<pre>` → fenced, lists, tables, links).
- **`web_search` tool** via Brave Search API with country/language/date filters.
- **`context_status` tool**: kin's current token-usage estimate.
- **`list_processes` / `kill_process`** tools for managed background processes.
- **Scheduled cron wake-ups** via Windows Task Scheduler. Per-kin entries; subprocess (`hearthkin_cron.py`) handles both inject-when-running and journal-only modes.
- **JSONL conversation persistence** — `conversation.jsonl` append-only per turn replaces full-rewrite `conversation.json`.
- **Tool-call round-trip persistence** so the kin sees its own past tool calls on subsequent turns.
- **Usage tab** in the main notebook between Chat and Preferences.
- **Anti-mode-collapse sampling knobs**: `presence_penalty`, `frequency_penalty`, `min_p` exposed as sliders.
- **Single-instance enforcement** + installer's RestartManager integration.
- **`EditKinDialog` refactored into a six-tab `wx.Notebook`** (Identity / Model && generation / Memory / Tools / Telegram / Cron). Voice tab added later in the v0.2.x cycle, bringing it to seven; v0.2.35 adds Chat as the eighth.

### Changed (highlights)
- **Modularization pass**: monolithic `hearthkin.pyw` (~5300 lines) broken into seven focused modules (`telegram_bot`, `kin_persistence`, `dialogs`, `audio`, `model_utils`, `chat_helpers`, plus the `tools/` subsystem).
- **Provider-agnostic `tool_calls.arguments` normalization** in `chat()` — Ollama wants dicts, OpenRouter wants JSON strings; either shape can land on disk, dispatcher coerces on every outbound.
- **Sentence-by-sentence streaming** in 1-on-1 mode — content chunks accumulate into a buffer, painted live up to the last sentence boundary. One coherent text-change event per sentence (readable as it streams, no NVDA flood).
- **`hearthkin_think.py` → `hearthkin.pyw`** (no-console-flash on Windows double-click via `pythonw.exe`).
- **Status bar shows context, not just notifications** — `kin · model · pct% cap` with `(close)` / `(truncating)` markers at 85% / 100%.
- **Cold-start hint** at 8s of no first chunk (cross-provider: Ollama unload, OR cold TLS, Anthropic prompt-cache miss).

### Fixed (highlights)
- **NVDA streaming flooding cascade** — switched all `AppendText`-per-chunk to atomic-paint at turn-end. Status changed from "thinking" to "typing" because some models really do reason.
- **Reply/reasoning split bug** in 1-on-1 — flushing order ensured the painted sentences land contiguously, with the reasoning beneath them as a single block.
- **OpenRouter reasoning-toggle**: send `reasoning: {enabled: True}` instead of `{max_tokens: 0}` (which can silently disable thinking on Anthropic).
- **Empty-reply diagnostics** (`empty_replies.log`) always-on, regardless of general logging toggle.
- **`Ollama tool-call str-args fallback`** for smaller models (gpt-oss:20b et al) that return tool-call arguments as JSON-encoded strings.

## v0.1.0 – v0.1.2 — early 2026

Initial public-ish releases. Single-file `hearthkin.pyw` (~5300 lines at the peak). wxPython UI, Ollama-only backend, multi-kin chat, rooms, memory distillation, anti-impersonation cleanup chain, Telegram bot, NVDA-first accessibility design.

---

## Format

This changelog follows the spirit of [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/), though entries lean toward describing root cause + mechanism alongside the user-visible change — most fixes are subtle enough that future-readers (and future-Claude instances picking up the codebase cold) need the WHY along with the WHAT.

Versioning is semver-ish: minor version bumps for substantial new features (the v0.2.0 voice + tools release and the v0.3.0 image-attachments + webcam release were both such), patch bumps for everything else.
